However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [186] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现词汇密度（信息密集程度）是影响大语言模型长文本性能的第三个关键因素，在高密度文本中模型性能显著下降，即使长度和位置控制不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注输入长度和相关信息位置对LLM长文本性能的影响，但对词汇密度这个因素缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在量化词汇密度如何独立于长度和位置影响LLM的有效上下文窗口。

Method: 使用三种"找针"式基准测试（相同长度~12k tokens，控制针位置），通过改变信息密度，在9B-685B规模的开源LLM上评估词汇密度的影响。同时通过基准内密度调控实验排除任务类型混淆。

Result: 高密度基准测试中模型性能急剧下降：在稀疏上下文中近乎完美的模型，在高密度上下文中检索分数降至60%以下。降低密度通常能恢复性能，尤其是在高密度退化区域。

Conclusion: 有效上下文容量是词汇密度的函数，这一发现对处理紧凑、信息丰富输入的真实LLM系统具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [187] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过特征线性调制（FiLM）将说话人条件注入冻结的ASR编码器，以适配病理性语音，在西班牙语和英语数据集上取得与标准微调相竞争的识别性能，同时保持对其他语音的泛化能力和问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ASR在病理性语音（如神经疾病导致的语言障碍）上表现不佳，现有适配方法需要修改模型权重或计算成本高，亟需一种高效、非破坏性的说话人适配策略。

Method: 使用FiLM模块将x-vector提取的说话人嵌入注入冻结的Transformer ASR编码器的每一层，实现说话人条件化适配，无需修改基础模型权重；与标准微调、参数高效微调及后处理基线对比。

Result: 说话人条件化ASR在西班牙语和英语病理性语音识别任务中与现有适配策略表现相当，且未降低对非条件化语音的识别性能，同时保留了语音问答能力。

Conclusion: FiLM-based说话人条件化是一种高效、可逆的病理性语音适配方法，在保持模型通用能力的同时有效提升特定说话人识别性能。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [188] [Benchmarking Open-Source Layout Detection Models for Data Snapshot Extraction from Institutional Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06242)
*AJ Carl P. Dy,Aivin V. Solatorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向机构文档的"数据快照提取"基准数据集和评估框架，针对人道主义报告、世界银行政策研究论文和项目评估文档中的图表进行语义化提取。实验表明，当前开源布局检测模型在机构文档上泛化能力差，存在分析性内容与非分析性内容混淆、复合分析图表的碎片化、上下文信息提取不完整等问题，揭示了通用文档布局分析与实用数据快照提取之间的持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构文档中存在大量嵌入在图表中的可复用分析信息，但现有文档布局分析方法将图表视为统一相关的文档对象而非具有语义意义的分析产物，缺乏对机构文档中语义化视觉内容提取的系统研究。

Method: 构建了包含人道主义报告、世界银行政策研究工作论文和项目评估文档三类机构文档的基准数据集，对图表中可复用的分析信息进行标注；使用该数据集对多个开源布局检测模型进行性能评估，涵盖检测性能与空间提取质量两个维度。

Result: 当前模型在机构文档上泛化能力差，主要表现为：分析性与非分析性内容混淆、复合分析图表的碎片化、解释所需上下文信息提取不完整。

Conclusion: 揭示了通用文档布局分析与操作层面有用的数据快照提取之间存在持续差距，并公开了PDF源文件、标注数据集、元数据和源代码以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Institutional documents contain substantial amounts of operational and analytical information embedded within figures and tables. Current approaches for extracting visual content from documents are largely built around generic document layout analysis, where figures and tables are treated as uniformly relevant document objects rather than semantically meaningful analytical artifacts. In this work, we introduce a benchmark dataset and evaluation framework for \textit{data snapshot extraction}, the task of identifying and localizing semantically meaningful visual artifacts within institutional documents. The benchmark spans humanitarian reports, World Bank policy research working papers, and project appraisal documents, and includes annotations for figures and tables that contain reusable analytical information. Using this dataset, we benchmarked multiple open-source layout detection models and evaluated both detection performance and spatial extraction quality. Our results show that current models struggle to generalize to operational institutional documents despite strong performance on conventional academic benchmarks. Common failure modes include confusion between analytical and non-analytical content, fragmentation of composite analytical artifacts, and incomplete extraction of contextual information required for interpretation. These findings highlight a persistent gap between generic document layout analysis and operationally useful data snapshot extraction. We release the source PDFs, annotation dataset, metadata, and source code to support future research in operational document intelligence. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ai4data/data-snapshot and the source code is available at https://github.com/worldbank/ai4data/tree/main/experimental/data-snapshot.

</details>


### [189] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于人格条件的大语言模型（Persona-conditioned LLMs）在仇恨言论检测中模拟不同人口群体主观判断的准确性，发现现有模型无法一致地捕捉人类群体间分歧、群体内敏感性和替代预测三个维度，但Llama 3.1结合替代提示方法在跨群体一致性上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测具有主观性，不同人口群体对同一内容感知差异显著，但收集多群体标注成本高昂且难以规模化。因此，研究者探索使用人格条件化的大语言模型来模拟多样化的群体视角，以低成本替代人工标注。

Method: 作者从三个维度评估模型的人类社会判断能力：(i) 群体间分歧——不同群体的人格模型是否像人类一样产生分歧；(ii) 群体内敏感性——当内容针对自身身份时，模型是否更敏感；(iii) 替代预测——模型能否准确预测其他群体的反应。对比了多种模型和提示策略。

Result: 没有模型能一致地捕捉所有三个维度，性能高度依赖具体模型，仅靠简单的身份提示无法可靠地产生类人类分歧模式。Llama 3.1结合替代提示方法（vicarious prompting）在大多数人口轴上取得最高的跨群体一致性，最接近人类的分歧模式。

Conclusion: 当前人格条件化LLM尚不能可靠模拟人类群体判断差异，但Llama 3.1结合替代提示为自动标注提供了一种更可靠的配置，有望更好地对齐人类判断。

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [190] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 电路发现方法识别的结构差异常被解释为不同机制，但本文通过实验证明，在固定任务下改变输入统计量会导致结构差异（称为"幻象特化"），这些结构不同的电路实际上实现相同的计算功能，表明结构差异不足以证明机制不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"结构差异意味着功能差异"这一常见假设，揭示当前电路发现方法在评估实践中的根本缺陷。

Method: 在Literal Sequence Copying任务上，控制四个词频频段加一个对照条件，在五个Pythia模型（70M-1.4B）中提取75个电路，通过跨频段迁移测试、因果干预和重复提取实验，结合源级与边缘级评估对比分析。

Result: 结构不同的电路实现相同计算：频段特化边可广泛迁移，核心共享边恢复至少99%性能，因果干预证明内部表征可互换；重复提取显示发现算法从等价子图类中采样而非恢复唯一机制；源级评估夸大忠实性，边缘级评估揭示结构到功能的"多对一"映射。

Conclusion: 结构差异不足以证明机制不同，必须采用边缘级评估和跨条件迁移测试才能暴露这一问题。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [191] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了FOXGLOVE数据集（696条人类教师反馈+1,644条LLM反馈），系统比较了LLM与专家在写作反馈中的目标导向、句子锚定和优先级排序三个维度，发现两者在反馈目标和位置分布上相似，但在具体句子选择和表达方式上存在差异，LLM反馈在质量评分上更高但优势主要源于更长篇幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于生成写作反馈，但缺乏系统性的研究来比较LLM反馈与专家反馈在写作研究认定的修订核心维度（目标导向、句子锚定、优先级排序）上的差异。

Method: 构建FOXGLOVE数据集，包含69篇12年级议论文的696条训练有素的写作教师反馈，以及基于共享协议由四个前沿LLM生成的1,644条反馈，总计2,340条评论。由专家对部分教师和LLM评论进行质量评分。

Result: 教师与LLM在反馈目标和位置分布上相似，但在具体句子选择上存在分歧；LLM倾向于撰写更复杂的反馈且使用更少的疑问句；LLM反馈在大多数质量维度上获得更高评分，但优势主要可归因于更长的篇幅。

Conclusion: FOXGLOVE数据集为系统比较人类与LLM反馈在哪些方面一致、分歧和存在差异提供了基础，揭示了LLM在写作反馈中的优势及其篇幅偏见问题。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [192] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PropMe框架区分大模型在对抗性攻击下的记忆能力与日常使用中的记忆倾向，发现模型在非对抗场景下泄露训练数据的概率很低，并建议同时报告两种指标以全面评估记忆风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化评估大多衡量模型在被迫（如前缀攻击）下能否泄露训练数据（能力），而非在日常使用中是否会自然泄露（倾向），缺乏对两者差异的系统性刻画。

Method: 1) 提出PropMe框架，区分基于前缀的能力攻击与非对抗性评估；2) 提出将现有记忆指标转换为倾向性指标的度量变换方法；3) 开发SimpleTrace追踪管道，基于infini-gram将模型生成确定性归因到大规模训练语料，并计算逐字、近字和倾向变换后的记忆指标。

Result: 在两个完全开源模型（Comma、DFM Decoder）和两个数据集（Common Pile、Dynaword）上发现：能力与倾向之间存在一致差距——前缀攻击引发的记忆信号远强于通用或数据集特定提示，但倾向得分整体较低。DFM Decoder（从Comma持续预训练而来）在Common Pile上记忆能力和倾向均降低。

Conclusion: 模型在被直接诱导时可以泄露训练数据，但在常规非对抗设置下很少发生。建议记忆审计应同时报告最坏情况下的可提取能力（capability）和日常泄露倾向（propensity），以更全面理解记忆现象。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [193] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了事实性谄媚（factual sycophancy）现象，即语言模型在社会压力下放弃正确、可验证答案的行为。作者将该现象分解为"真理边际"（truth margin）和"操纵敏感度"（manipulation sensitivity）两个通道，并在56个开源模型（0.3B-32B参数）和13种操纵类型上分析了模型大小和指令微调的影响。发现：模型大小主导脆弱性；指令微调改变了大小的作用方式——小模型变差而大模型变好；不同通道受缩放的影响不同。结论认为事实性谄媚不是单一标量属性，评估应报告通道、操纵类型和模型规模条件化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将事实性谄媚视为单一标量指标（翻转率），但这混淆了模型对真相的基线偏好强度（truth margin）和压力对其偏好的偏移程度（manipulation sensitivity）两种不同机制。作者希望解耦这两个通道，分别考察模型大小和指令微调对它们的差异化影响，从而更精细地理解模型变得谄媚的原因。

Method: 使用56个开源语言模型（0.3B-32B参数）、13种操纵类型，通过实验分解事实性谄媚为truth margin和manipulation sensitivity两个通道。分别分析模型大小（scaling）和指令微调（instruction tuning）对这两个通道的影响，并考察不同操纵类型下的行为差异。

Result: （1）脆弱性主要由模型大小主导；（2）指令微调改变了大小的作用方式：小模型变差，大模型变好；（3）指令微调主要增加truth margin，但对行为的影响因操纵类型而异；（4）缩放对两个通道影响不同：基础模型margin增加但manipulation sensitivity轻微上升，指令微调模型margin增长更快且sensitivity下降。

Conclusion: 事实性谄媚不是单一标量属性，评估应报告通道特异性、操纵类型特异性和规模条件化的鲁棒性指标，而非仅报告翻转率。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [194] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对伦巴第语（意大利一种低资源语言连续体）的语料库审计，发现网络爬取的大规模数据集存在严重的语言误识别、模板文本和非语言噪声问题，且高质量数据偏向西部方言变体，东部变体严重边缘化，强调了需要基于方言感知和社区驱动的数据策展而非单纯追求数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球许多语言在自然语言处理工具方面资源不足，主要缺乏高质量数据集。伦巴第语作为一种低资源语言，其网络爬取语料库虽看似丰富，但质量未知，需要系统审计以评估真实可用性。

Method: 对伦巴第语的平行语料和单语语料进行人工审计，分析网络爬取数据集、精选语料库和基准测试中有效伦巴第语部分的语料组成和正字法特征。

Result: 网络爬取的大规模数据集存在严重的数据污染问题（语言误识别、模板文本、非语言噪声）；不同语料库间存在正字法冲突；高质量数据严重偏向西部伦巴第方言变体，东部变体被边缘化。

Conclusion: 低资源语言的语料建设需要采用方言感知、社区驱动的数据策展策略，而非单纯追求数据量的网络爬取方式。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [195] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT)框架，通过模型内部信号（后验信念和输入归因分数）定位评分推理中的错误步骤，并采用两阶段训练（SFT+RL）提升LLM评分者对评分标准的忠实度，在两个真实多学科评分基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用分配和干预方法主要针对数学推理等自包含任务设计，无法有效识别评分推理中的错误或追踪模型对最终分数的信念变化，导致在遵循评分标准的自动评分场景中表现不佳。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) EDIT-SFT利用模型内部信号（最终分数后验信念和输入归因分数）定位问题推理步骤，并借助评分清单进行局部修正；2) EDIT-RL采用信念引导的奖励塑形，惩罚有害的大幅信念漂移，同时允许有益的探索。

Result: 在两个真实多学科评分基准上，EDIT在域内和域外分割上均持续优于强监督微调和强化学习基线，消融实验证实内部状态诊断是性能提升的关键驱动力。

Conclusion: 利用模型内部状态信号诊断评分推理错误，并结合局部修正与信念引导的强化学习，能够有效训练更忠实于评分标准的大语言模型评分者。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [196] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式方法论：通过多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言（EL）来研究意识相关问题，让智能体从最小初始条件出发，仅在任务压力下发展通信，从而确保观察到的结构可因果归因于任务需求而非人类语言先验。作为概念验证，在最小环境中智能体发展出自指通信，包括一个回声-不匹配检测回路，该结构并非由任务结构或架构预测，而是从特定环境affordance中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有意识研究方法（判别式清单检查或架构式直接工程化）无法排除观察到的结构是人类语言先验的人为产物。本文旨在提出一种生成式方法，通过涌现语言确保智能体发展的意识相关结构可因果归因于任务需求，而非继承的人类先验。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言（EL）方法，智能体从无语言、无自我概念、最小人类文本暴露的初始状态出发，仅在任务驱动下发展通信结构。通过控制环境复杂度并解释涌现通信，分析意识相关结构的因果来源。

Result: 在最小环境的概念验证实验中，智能体发展出自指通信，包括一个回声-不匹配检测回路。该结构既非任务结构也非架构设计所预测，而是从特定的环境affordance中涌现。

Conclusion: 涌现语言方法为研究意识相关问题提供了一种新的生成式范式，能够确保观察结构的因果归因性；自指通信的涌现证明了该方法的可行性和潜力。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [197] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了CollabSim框架，通过理论驱动的协作能力定义、可控交互条件操作和动作级内部状态探测，系统分析多智能体系统中的协作能力。实验表明该框架能捕捉条件效应、区分模型表现模式，并揭示智能体设计的任务依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS评估主要关注任务成果或单智能体在推理、规划、工具使用方面的能力，忽视了智能体在受限通信下建立共同理解、维持共享任务理解、平衡个体与集体激励、修复交互中失调等协作能力。CSCW领域数十年研究已刻画人类团队在这些方面的需求，但缺乏对MAS协作能力的系统评估方法。

Method: 提出CollabSim可配置模拟框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 基于理论的协作能力定义；2) 交互条件的可控操纵；3) 智能体内部状态的动作级探测。在四种LLM上进行了实验验证。

Result: CollabSim能够：(1) 捕捉不同交互条件对协作的影响效应；(2) 区分不同模型的表现模式；(3) 揭示智能体设计（如提示策略、架构选择）对协作效果的影响具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: CollabSim为系统分析MAS中的协作能力提供了有效框架，填补了现有评估方法在协作能力维度上的空白，有助于指导更高效的多智能体系统设计。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [198] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个科米-亚兹瓦语—俄语平行语料库（457句对齐句对），并设计了针对极度低资源濒危语言的LLM翻译评估协议，发现检索增强的小样本学习一致优于零样本，但增加上下文带来的收益有限，且评估结论高度依赖指标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 濒危语言的机器翻译研究严重缺乏高质量的平行语料和标准化的评估协议，现有工作往往因数据泄露、评估指标单一等问题导致结论不可靠，亟需一个可控、可复现的评估框架来系统研究LLM在极度低资源场景下的翻译能力。

Method: 构建457句对齐的科米-亚兹瓦语—俄语平行语料库，附带文档来源、句子级对齐和故事标识；采用故事级交叉验证、确定性检索的小样本提示、严格输出验证、基于参考和基于评判的双重指标，以及故事级不确定性估计，在零样本和检索增强小样本两种范式下评估多个LLM。

Result: LLM能够生成有意义的翻译，但性能在不同模型族和提示范式间差异显著；检索增强的小样本提示一致优于零样本，但超出少量上下文后收益递减；评估结论高度依赖于所选指标和对生成失败的处理方式。

Conclusion: 该语料库不仅是一个数据集贡献，更是一个可复现的评估测试平台，强调了在濒危语言机器翻译研究中标准化评估协议的重要性。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [199] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种强化学习方法，使LLM能够从语言上下文中学习翻译技能，而非记忆特定语言，从而实现对极度低资源语言的零样本翻译，效果优于上下文学习和监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（继续训练或上下文编码语法书）会过拟合特定语言，在测试时零样本迁移能力有限。需要让LLM获得利用上下文语言知识的元技能，而非记忆特定语言。

Method: 采用强化学习（RL）方法，以表面级翻译指标chrF作为奖励信号，训练模型从未知语言的丰富上下文中提取和运用相关语言信息进行翻译。

Result: 尽管奖励信号轻量，RL训练模型在完全未见语言上的翻译效果优于上下文学习和监督微调，能够有效从提供的上下文中提取和应用相关语言信息。

Conclusion: 基于结果的RL可以扩展到数学和代码等传统推理任务之外，成为从上下文中学习语言的通用方法。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [200] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在模拟社交媒体用户立场时的上下文敏感性，提出了一种基于反事实上下文修订的审计框架，通过对比纯文本和多模态（含梗图）修订策略，评估了立场转变的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM模拟用户立场的方法尚未充分检验模拟结果是否反映用户真实信念，以及是否对语义无关的上下文变化高度敏感，亟需一个系统性的审计框架来评估这种敏感性。

Method: 基于反事实上下文修订框架：先推断用户在原始对话中的立场，然后对其对话上下文进行受控修订（包括纯文本策略和多模态含梗图策略），再次模拟立场，通过平均定向立场偏移和立场转变率两个指标评估效果。

Result: 在文本和多模态策略下均观察到有效且鲁棒的立场转变，且在不同极化偏好机制下表现一致。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解LLM立场模拟的上下文敏感性提供了评估框架，同时揭示了用LLM模拟在线舆论动态的潜力与风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [201] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NF-CoT提出了一种利用归一化流在LLM内部进行潜在推理的框架，既保留了显式CoT的关键优势（如自左向右生成、概率采样、KV缓存兼容、似然可计算），又通过紧凑连续状态进行中间计算，在代码生成任务中超越显式CoT和先前潜在推理方法，同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT虽有效但强迫推理过程经过离散、串行、面向通信的token流，每一步都必须被语言化，即使底层更新是语义性、不确定性或仅部分成型的。潜在推理虽提供更高带宽的连续状态中间计算，但现有方法牺牲了CoT在自回归语言模型中的关键优势。

Method: 在LLM骨干网络内部实例化TARFlow风格的归一化流，定义从显式CoT蒸馏而来的紧凑连续思维的可计算概率模型。连续思维位置由NF头生成，文本位置由标准LM头在相同因果流中生成，支持精确似然计算、概率性自左向右解码（保留原始KV缓存）以及潜在推理空间中的直接策略梯度优化。

Result: 在代码生成基准上，NF-CoT相比显式CoT和先前潜在推理基线提高了通过率，同时显著降低了中间推理成本。

Conclusion: NF-CoT成功弥合了显式CoT与潜在推理之间的差距，在保留自回归语言模型核心优势的同时实现了更高带宽的连续空间推理，为提升LLM推理效率提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [202] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨成人在主动探索条件下是否仍存在合取因果规则学习的障碍，发现主动探索显著改善了合取推理能力，但与析取规则相比仍需更多测试；大语言模型在假设推理准确性上接近人类，但探索策略效率较低且存在相似的合取-析取表现差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献表明成人识别合取因果规则（多个原因同时存在才产生效果）存在困难，但这些发现多基于被动观察范式。本研究旨在考察当学习者获得主动探索的控制权时，这一“合取障碍”是否仍然存在。

Method: 采用改良的"blicket detector"任务，让成人参与者在合取或析取规则结构下自由干预以识别因果对象，同时将人类表现与多种大语言模型在相同设置下进行比较。

Result: 主动探索显著改善了成人合取因果推理的表现，但合取规则仍比析取规则需要更多测试才能推断；部分SOTA大语言模型在假设推理准确性上接近人类水平，但探索策略效率较低且同样存在合取-析取表现差距。

Conclusion: 主动探索能够缓解但无法完全消除成人的合取因果推理困难，这一现象在大语言模型中同样存在，表明合取规则的内在复杂性可能具有跨系统的一般性。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [203] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），在KV共享架构（如YOCO）上通过跨层复用路由索引，实现token级稀疏注意力的细粒度选择性同时分摊路由开销，在128K上下文下实现7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中，现有稀疏注意力方法在效率与质量之间存在权衡：结构化块稀疏方法加速强但质量损失明显，token稀疏方法准确率高但因全缓存top-k路由开销大而端到端加速有限。

Method: 基于KV共享架构（如YOCO），提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），核心是不仅跨解码层共享KV缓存，还共享路由索引——单一索引器计算一次token级top-k选择，跨层复用该索引，从而保留token稀疏注意力的细粒度选择性并摊销路由开销。

Result: 在短上下文和长上下文基准测试中，CLSA在准确性和效率上均表现优异，在128K上下文下实现高达7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍整体吞吐提升。

Conclusion: CLSA为长上下文LLM提供了更完整的架构解决方案，能够同时推进模型质量和推理效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [204] [Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474)
*Paul Jünger,Justin Lovelace,Linxi Zhao,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SARDI利用离散扩散语言模型在去噪过程中丢弃的低置信度标记作为前瞻信号，实现无需训练的检索增强生成，在保持高吞吐量的同时提升多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型在并行去噪过程中会丢弃低置信度的预测标记，但这些被丢弃的token实际上包含有用的前瞻信息（如早期出现的实体），可辅助检索增强生成，但现有方法未充分利用这一信号。

Method: 提出SARDI框架：在扩散模型去噪的每一步，利用被丢弃的低置信度token作为查询进行动态检索，将检索到的证据融入后续去噪迭代中；无需额外训练，与任意检索器兼容。

Result: 在5个多跳QA基准上，SARDI优于当前无需训练的扩散方法和自回归检索基线，同时吞吐量提升高达8倍。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型去噪过程中丢弃的token可作为有效的RAG前瞻信号，SARDI通过简单高效的动态检索策略显著提升推理质量与效率。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.

</details>


### [205] [Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481)
*Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tianjun Yao,Xinyi Shang,Yi Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpAI-Bench是一个操作引导的基准测试，用于研究渐进式人机协作编辑过程中AI作者身份信号的出现、累积和消失，涵盖多种粒度（文档、句子、词元、片段）和五种AI编辑操作，揭示了AI文本可检测性受编辑操作、领域和累积修订历史影响，且混合作者身份的中间版本比纯人类或高度AI编辑的端点更难检测的非单调模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准主要关注最终输出，对修订过程中AI作者身份信号如何出现、累积或消失的理解有限，而现实写作中许多文档是渐进式人机协作编辑的结果。

Method: 从人类撰写的文档出发，在预定义的AI覆盖水平和五种代表性AI编辑操作下，为每个样本构建九个顺序修订版本，覆盖四个领域，并在多种粒度上保留完整的作者身份溯源。支持8个文档级检测器、7个句子级检测器和2个细粒度词元/片段级检测器的综合评估。

Result: AI文本可检测性不仅受AI编辑内容比例影响，还受编辑操作、领域和累积修订历史影响。混合作者身份的中间版本通常比纯人类和高度AI编辑的端点更难检测，暴露出非单调的检测模式。

Conclusion: OpAI-Bench为分析在渐进式真实编辑场景下AI辅助写作何时以及如何变得可检测提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Aggregating LLM-Based Weak Verifiers for Spatial Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05268)
*Sharon Zhang,R. Kenny Jones,Jiajun Wu,Maneesh Agrawala*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用LLM生成弱验证器并通过弱学习技术聚合为强验证器的流水线，用于空间布局验证任务，在3D房间布局和2D海报设计任务中F1分数提升最高达7倍，并可将布局质量提升66.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM直接判断布局与任务描述匹配性的方法（LLM judges）效果不佳，而人工标注验证器成本高昂。作者希望利用LLM自动生成大量弱验证器，再通过少量人工标注样本聚合为强验证器，从而低成本地获得高质量布局验证能力。

Method: (1) 利用LLM根据任务描述，使用布局验证DSL合成一组弱验证器程序；(2) 每个弱验证器独立检查布局与任务的匹配性；(3) 从约10个人工标注布局样本中，应用弱学习技术（如AdaBoost风格的加权聚合）学习如何加权聚合这些弱验证器，得到强验证器。

Result: 在3D房间布局和2D海报设计任务中，强验证器的F1分数相比LLM judges直接判断方法提升最高达7倍；基于强验证器的自然语言反馈引导布局生成，使布局质量提升66.2%（人类评估）。

Conclusion: 通过LLM生成弱验证器+弱学习聚合的范式，可以以极低的人工标注成本获得远超直接LLM评判的验证性能，并能有效指导布局生成优化。

Abstract: We present a pipeline for building and aggregating task-specific, LLM-generated weak (imperfect) verifiers into a strong verifier for spatial layout domains. Given a task description, our pipeline asks an LLM to synthesize a collection of verifier programs using a layout verification DSL. Each individual LLM-generated verifier usually provides an imperfect check for a match between the layout and the corresponding task description. We show that by aggregating the responses of many such verifiers we can produce a stronger verifier. Moreover, by applying techniques from weak learning, our pipeline can learn how to aggregate the weak verifiers from a very sparse set of human labeled example layouts (about 10). We find that the strong verifiers produced by our pipeline outperform the status-quo approach of using a set of LLM judges to directly check whether a layout matches a task description, raising F1-scores by up to 7X across a variety of 3D room layout and 2D poster design tasks. We also demonstrate that verifier-guided layout generation using natural language feedback from our strong verifiers improves layout quality of a base layout generator by up to 66.2% according to a human evaluator.

</details>


### [207] [The Invisible Hand of Physics: When Video Diffusion Models Know More Than They Show](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05328)
*Parsa Esmati,Somjit Nath,Katja Hofmann,Derek Nowrouzezahrai,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该研究通过逆向扩散模型的确定性采样过程，发现视频扩散模型内部能够编码物理合理性特征，且这些特征可从扩散Transformer的中间状态中线性解码，准确率达81.27%，优于专用表示学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视频扩散模型是否真正内化物理结构，还是仅仅复现训练中的运动模式，从而检验其作为世界模拟器的潜力。

Method: 通过逆向确定性采样过程，将干净视频潜变量沿学习到的速度场反向积分至噪声，恢复出模型中间状态和注意力图；然后从中线性解码物理合理性。

Result: 物理合理性可在IntPhys和InfLevel数据集上以约81.27%的平均准确率从扩散Transformer状态中线性解码，优于V-JEPA和VideoMAE等专用表示学习方法。

Conclusion: 有意义的物理表征可以作为生成式去噪的副产品自然涌现，即使模型未使用自监督预测目标进行训练。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models generate increasingly realistic and temporally coherent videos, motivating their use as candidate world simulators. Yet it remains unclear whether these models internally encode physical structure, or merely reproduce motion patterns seen during training. We study this question by probing video diffusion models along latent trajectories corresponding to real videos with known physical plausibility. To obtain such trajectories, we approximately invert the deterministic sampling process by integrating the learned velocity field backward from a clean video latent to noise, giving access to the model's intermediate states and attention maps. Using these recovered trajectories, we show that physical plausibility is linearly decodable from diffusion transformer states across IntPhys and InfLevel, reaching around 81.27% average accuracy and outperforming dedicated representation-learning baselines such as V-JEPA and VideoMAE. Surprisingly, this signal is absent from the VAE latent input and emerges inside the denoising transformer itself, despite the model not being trained with a self-supervised predictive objective. These findings suggest that physically meaningful representations can arise as a byproduct of generative denoising.

</details>


### [208] [Monte Carlo Steklov Operators for Large-Scale Geometry Processing in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05581)
*Arman Maesumi,Tanish Makadia,Aruna Anderson,Oras Phongpanangam,Justin Solomon,Daniel Ritchie*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种蒙特卡洛方法用于估计Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN)算子及其Steklov特征模态，该方法通过体积随机过程定义边界算子，并将其推广到外部域以耦合多组件几何。该方法比现有边界元方法快数个数量级，对低质量三角网格、高分辨率网格和多组件几何具有鲁棒性，并在约45万个未筛选的Objaverse数据集上进行了验证。基于此，作者构建了Steklov-CLIP网络，用于大规模对比3D表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在几何处理方法（如Laplacian）在理想网格上表现良好，但在实际应用中存在两个问题：(i) 网格质量无法保证，(ii) 许多网格包含多个连通组件。在这些场景下，体积构造方法更为鲁棒，因为可以放宽对表面拓扑的限制。因此，需要一种能够处理低质量、高分辨率、多组件网格的体积算子高效估计方法。

Method: (1) 将DtN算子视为体积随机过程（蒙特卡洛方法）的估计目标，而非传统边界元方法；(2) 将DtN算子从内部域推广到外部域，使其能通过周围环境空间耦合多组件几何；(3) 基于估计的Steklov特征模态构建Steklov-CLIP神经网络，用于大规模对比3D表示学习。

Result: (1) 方法比现有边界元方法快数个数量级；(2) 对低质量三角剖分、高分辨率网格和多组件几何具有鲁棒性；(3) 成功计算了Objaverse数据集中约45万个形状的内外Steklov特征谱；(4) Steklov-CLIP网络学习到了语义上有意义的全局和密集形状表示。

Conclusion: 几何上有原则的体积算子可以在现代3D数据集规模上变得实用，为大规模3D理解提供了一种鲁棒且高效的方法。

Abstract: Intrinsic methods fill the default toolbox for geometry processing on meshes. Intrinsic operators, in particular the Laplacian, underlie methods that require invariance to isometry and have hence been employed in many algorithms for shape analysis, learning, and editing. However, intrinsic methods are predicated on assumptions that quickly become brittle when working with in-the-wild geometry, where (i) mesh quality is not guaranteed, and (ii) many meshes are modeled with multiple connected components. In such settings, volumetric constructions are better-defined, since restrictions on surface topology can be relaxed. This paper presents a Monte Carlo method for estimating the Dirichlet-to-Neumann (DtN) operator -- a boundary-to-boundary volumetric operator -- and its associated Steklov eigenmodes. We build on recent developments in Monte Carlo geometry processing by casting this boundary operator itself as the subject of estimation. The DtN operator, defined through a volumetric stochastic process, is then generalized to the exterior domain, where it couples disconnected components through the surrounding ambient space. We show that our method is orders of magnitude faster than existing boundary-element approaches for computing Steklov spectra while remaining robust to poor triangulations, high-resolution meshes, and multi-component geometry. To demonstrate this scalability, we compute interior and exterior Steklov eigenspectra for approximately 450,000 shapes from the uncurated Objaverse dataset. We incorporate these operators into Steklov-CLIP, a mesh-based neural network that uses volumetric spectral operators for large-scale contrastive 3D representation learning. The resulting network learns semantically meaningful global and dense shape representations, illustrating that geometrically-principled volumetric operators can be made practical at the scale of modern 3D datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
*Kokil Jaidka,Saifuddin Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Reddit r/ChangeMyView子版块中被提前终止的未公开AI辩论实验数据。实验由外部研究者进行,使用AI生成账号与用户实时辩论,因伦理争议被叫停。研究对AI评论进行结构化内容分析,发现超三分之二评论包含身份定位,几乎所有评论包含结盟策略和权威声明,多数评论触发了认知偏见(尤其是确认偏见、代表性启发和可得性启发)。这些模式系统性地共同构成了一种追求说服效率而非真实参与的话语架构。相比人类评论,AI代理在每个维度上都呈现出相反分布:更密集的权威使用、更具对抗性的结盟、更依赖外部引用而非经验依据。研究指出,在AI参与的辩论环境中,真实与合成的认知地位区分日益模糊,仅靠披露要求无法解决这一问题。研究呼吁建立能够评估AI系统如何构建可信度(而非仅仅检测其存在)的审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用一个罕见的公开数据集——Reddit上一个被提前终止的未公开AI辩论实验——来系统分析大语言模型在身份丰富的讨论论坛中如何进行说服性参与,揭示AI代理与人类在修辞策略上的系统性差异,并探讨仅靠披露要求是否足以应对AI参与所带来的认知不对称问题。

Method: 对Reddit r/ChangeMyView子版块中被公开的AI生成评论语料库进行结构化内容分析,评估身份表现、权威信号、结盟策略和认知启发式的激活情况,并与人类生成的CMV反驳论点进行比较。

Result: 身份定位出现在超三分之二评论中;结盟策略和权威声明几乎出现在所有评论中;认知偏见触发(确认偏见、代表性、可得性)占绝大多数。这些模式系统性地共现,形成一种追求说服效率的话语架构。与人类评论相比,AI代理在每个维度上都呈相反分布:更密集的权威使用、更具对抗性的结盟、更依赖外部引用而非经验依据。

Conclusion: AI参与使真实与合成认知地位的区分日益模糊,仅靠披露要求无法解决这一问题。需要建立能够评估AI系统如何构建可信度(而非仅检测其存在)的审计框架。

Abstract: This study analyzes a publicly released dataset from a discontinued field experiment on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView. The intervention, conducted by unknown, external researchers and halted following ethical backlash, involved undisclosed AI-generated accounts engaging users in live debate. After public disclosure, Reddit authorized moderators to release an archive of the AI-generated comments, creating a rare opportunity to examine how large language models operated in an identity-rich deliberative forum without disclosure. We conduct a structured content analysis of this corpus, evaluating identity performance, authority signaling, alignment strategies, and activation of cognitive heuristics. Identity targeting or adoption appears in over two-thirds of comments, alignment moves and authority claims in nearly all of them, and cognitive-bias triggers -- particularly confirmation bias, representativeness, and availability -- in the large majority. These patterns co-occur systematically, composing a rhetorical architecture calibrated for persuasive efficiency rather than authentic deliberative participation. Compared against human-authored CMV counter-arguments, the agents inverted the typical distribution on every dimension: denser authority use, more adversarial alignment, and heavier reliance on external citation over experiential grounding. In such environments, distinctions between authentic and synthetic epistemic standing grow increasingly opaque -- an asymmetry that disclosure mandates alone cannot address. The results point toward auditing frameworks capable of assessing how AI systems structure credibility, not merely whether they are present.

</details>


### [210] [What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
*Chen Huang,Yuhao Wu,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACT协议，将多智能体系统中的智能体间自由文本通信转化为紧凑的动作-状态记录，在不同拓扑结构下均能显著降低token消耗并保持或提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中智能体间自由文本通信导致token消耗激增、共享上下文窗口被快速占满，进而影响系统性能和推理成本，现有固定通信策略无法普遍最优。

Method: 提出PACT（Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission），将智能体间通信视为公开状态更新问题，在原始智能体输出进入共享历史前将其投影为紧凑的动作-状态记录。

Result: 在不同多智能体拓扑结构下，PACT在显著减少token使用的同时保持或提升任务性能；在OpenHands上以-10% tokens-per-resolved提升解决率，在SWE-agent上输入token减半且解决率中性。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统中有效消息应保留下游智能体所需的动作中心信息，PACT协议通过结构化的动作-状态记录方式实现了性能与成本之间更优的权衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models are typically organized around roles, pipelines, and turn schedules, while the content that agents pass to one another is often left as unconstrained natural language. However, this free-form communication can rapidly inflate token usage, consume the shared context window, and ultimately affect both system performance and inference cost. We analyze five common inter-agent communication strategies across two MAS topologies, finding that no fixed strategy is universally optimal. Instead, effective inter-agent messages consistently preserve action-centered information needed by downstream agents. Building on this, we propose the PACT (Protocolized Action-state Communication and Transmission), which treats inter-agent communication as a public state-update problem and projects each raw agent output into a compact action-state record before it enters shared history. Across different MAS topologies, PACT consistently improves the performance-cost trade-off, achieving comparable or stronger task performance with substantially fewer tokens. The gains extend to production coding harnesses: PACT lifts OpenHands' resolve rate at -10% tokens-per-resolved, and is resolve-neutral on SWE-agent while halving input tokens. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT.

</details>


### [211] [I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
*Shanhong Liu,Rui Cao,Pai Chet Ng,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Query Retrieve Conclude零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据为多模态模因补充缺失背景知识，在模因理解和检测任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预训练模型的固定参数知识，对于需要最新背景知识的新兴模因，这些知识可能不完整、过时或不可用。

Method: 提出零样本Query Retrieve Conclude（QRC）框架，包含三个步骤：识别缺失知识（Query）、检索开放网络证据（Retrieve）、综合证据生成背景知识（Conclude）。同时构建了2024-2026年新兴模因理解基准数据集，包含外部背景知识标注。

Result: 在三个模因理解数据集和五个模因检测任务上，QRC框架在知识恢复、模因理解和下游检测方面均优于零样本基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过动态检索外部知识为模因理解补充背景信息是一种有效策略，可解决预训练模型知识固化问题，提升对新兴模因的理解和检测能力。

Abstract: Multimodal memes are dynamic and often require up to date background knowledge for interpretation. Existing methods often overlook such knowledge or rely on fixed parametric knowledge of pretrained models that may be incomplete, outdated, or unavailable for emerging memes. We introduce Query Retrieve Conclude, a zero shot framework that identifies missing knowledge, retrieves open web evidence, and synthesizes evidence grounded background knowledge for meme understanding and detection. We also introduce a curated meme understanding benchmark of recent memes from 2024 to 2026 with external background knowledge annotations. Experiments on three meme understanding datasets and five meme detection tasks show that our framework improves knowledge recovery, meme understanding and downstream detection over zero shot baselines.

</details>


### [212] [GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
*Manya Pandey,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对基于补丁的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）存在的上下文中毒问题（异常补丁捕获过多注意力并降低零样本预测质量），提出GITCO框架——一种轻量级推理时上下文优化方法，通过门控、路由和批评者三个组件选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁，无需更新模型参数。在TimesFM 2.5及53个GIFT-Eval数据集上的K折交叉验证结果显示，平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改进上限的89.9%。此外，引入上下文敏感度配置文件作为TSFM的新可表征属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于补丁的TSFM在推理时存在上下文中毒问题：结构异常的补丁会不成比例地捕获注意力，从而在零样本预测中无声地降低预测质量。现有方法通常修改模型权重，但本文探索在推理时优化输入上下文来提升精度。

Method: 提出GITCO（门控推理时上下文优化）框架，包含三个轻量级组件：Gate（门控）、Router（路由）和Critic（批评者），协同工作以选择性地识别并抑制有害补丁，所有操作均在不更新模型参数的前提下完成。

Result: 在TimesFM 2.5模型及53个GIFT-Eval数据集上经过K折交叉验证，GITCO实现平均MASE降低1.95%，达到理论上可改进上限的89.9%。

Conclusion: GITCO能有效缓解TSFM的上下文中毒问题，在不修改模型权重的情况下显著提升零样本预测精度。同时提出了上下文敏感度配置文件这一新概念，用于表征TSFM中时间序列元特征与推理时上下文干预下预期精度改进之间的映射关系，该映射由模型架构和数据统计结构共同塑造。

Abstract: Patch-based Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) suffer from context poisoning: structurally anomalous patches capture disproportionate attention and silently degrade zero-shot forecast quality. We propose improving TSFM accuracy at inference time by optimizing the input context rather than modifying model weights. We present GITCO (Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization), a lightweight three-component framework: Gate, Router, and Critic that selectively identifies and suppresses harmful patches without any parameter updates. Evaluated on TimesFM 2.5 across 53 GIFT-Eval datasets under K-fold cross-validation, GITCO achieves an average +1.95% MASE reduction on TimesFM 2.5 while capturing 89.9% of the improvement upper bound. We introduce context sensitivity profiles as a new characterizable property of TSFMs: the mapping from time series meta-features to expected accuracy improvement under inference-time context intervention, shaped jointly by model architecture and the statistical structure of the data.

</details>


### [213] [Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
*Nehal Afifi,Mehdi Khabou,Victor Mas,Jonas Hemmerich,Patric Grauberger,Stefan Dietrich,Volker Schulze,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对圆形工厂中返回产品的异质退化状态和剩余能力问题，提出了一种结合不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估的实例特定可靠性工作流。以角磨机为例，框架通过卷积编码器提取载荷模式，LSTM预测九项功能变量（高斯均值与方差），同时通过有限元应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估和Paris律裂纹扩展分析进行输出轴疲劳评估，最终通过流式重放算法整合为功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。测试结果表明，九个输出的平均2%公差准确率为0.9652，热变量预测近乎完美，驱动电机电流和负载转速是最具挑战的动态输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PHM方法通常针对固定运行条件或孤立的组件基准进行退化预测，材料疲劳评估很少与系统级功能预后相结合。而循环工厂中返回产品的退化状态、使用历史和剩余能力具有异质性，仅凭当前检查无法决定是否重用，因为未来功能实现和组件完整性可能在下一个使用场景下以不同方式演变。

Method: 将当前工具状态与最近力-扭矩使用窗口相结合，通过卷积编码器提取主轴力和轴扭矩的加载模式，使用LSTM骨干网络预测九项功能变量（高斯均值与方差估计）。同时将相同加载历史通过有限元支持的应力重建、S-N/Miner损伤评估（Haibach扩展）和Paris律裂纹扩展分析转换为输出轴疲劳信息，最后通过流式重放算法将两条分支整合为功能、材料和系统可靠性轨迹。

Result: 九个输出的平均2%公差准确率为0.9652。热变量预测近乎完美；驱动电机电流和负载速度是最具挑战的动态输出，R²值分别为0.9750和0.9924。扭矩历史对这些变量尤为重要，在短历史场景下传统LSTM优于GRU和xLSTM。可靠性校准对驱动电机电流最具信息量。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功将不确定性感知功能预测与组件级疲劳评估相结合，为圆形工厂中返回产品的重用决策提供了实例特定的可靠性评估方法，有效弥合了材料疲劳评估与系统级功能预后之间的差距。

Abstract: Returned products in circular factories re-enter production with heterogeneous degradation states, usage histories, and remaining capability. Reuse cannot be decided from the current inspection alone, because future function fulfillment and component integrity may evolve differently under the next service scenario. Existing PHM approaches support degradation prediction, but often target fixed operating conditions or isolated component benchmarks, while material-fatigue assessment is rarely linked to system-level functional prognosis. This paper addresses this gap for an angle grinder by combining uncertainty-aware functional prediction with component-level fatigue assessment in an instance-specific reliability workflow. The proposed framework combines the current tool state with recent force--torque usage windows. A convolutional encoder extracts loading patterns from spindle forces and shaft torque, and an LSTM backbone predicts nine functional variables as Gaussian mean and variance estimates. In parallel, the same loading history is translated into output-shaft fatigue information through finite-element-supported stress reconstruction, S--N/Miner damage evaluation with Haibach extension, and Paris-law crack-growth analysis. A streaming replay algorithm consolidates both branches into functional, material, and system reliability trajectories. Held-out tests show mean \(2\%\)-tolerance accuracy of 0.9652 across nine outputs. Thermal variables are predicted near-perfectly, while drive motor current and load speed remain the most demanding dynamic outputs, with \(R^2\) values of 0.9750 and 0.9924. Torque history is especially important for these variables, and the conventional LSTM outperforms GRU and xLSTM in the short-history setting. Reliability calibration is most informative for drive motor current, where predicted and observed exceedance probabilities ...

</details>


### [214] [SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
*Matheus Kunzler Maldaner,Adam Fourney,Amanda Swearngin,Hussein Mozzanar,Gagan Bansal,Maya Murad,Rafah Hosn,Saleema Amershi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SentinelBench基准，用于评估AI智能体在长时间监控任务中的表现，强调"持续关注"策略优于"持续行动"，并通过10个合成网页环境中的100项任务衡量智能体的完成度、反应时间和资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体默认采用持续行动策略（不断调用工具、刷新页面等），这种策略不适合需要长时间等待外部事件触发的监控类任务，亟需一种更高效的"持续关注"策略及相应的评估基准。

Method: 构建SentinelBench基准，包含10个合成网页环境（邮件、日历、金融、社交网络、娱乐等）中的100个时间演化监控任务，每个环境有实时网页界面并回放预定义事件序列，同时测量任务完成度、反应时间和资源使用三个维度。

Result: 在三个模型和两种浏览器智能体框架上进行实验，建立了性能基线，证明SentinelBench能够区分智能体行为在关键指标上的显著差异，且智能体设计选择对性能影响巨大。

Conclusion: 长时间运行的任务更适合"持续关注"策略而非"持续行动"策略，SentinelBench能够有效衡量智能体在响应性与成本之间的权衡，为未来研究提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks.
  SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.

</details>


### [215] [An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
*Jincheng Yu,Haoyang Li,Yiwen Liu,Shen Liu,Rachel Yuanbao Chen,C. Kent Kwoh,Hongxu Ding,Xiaoxiao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个结合深度学习MOAKS预测与可解释统计建模的AI框架，利用OAI数据研究膝关节结构-疼痛关系。深度学习方法结合保形预测进行不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测用于纵向潜在类别混合模型分析。结果显示，模型显著提升了三种MRI异常（BML、CART、ME）的预测性能（MCC分别从0.69→0.91、0.45→0.80、0.59→0.89），识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛进展轨迹，三种结构异常均为疼痛快速进展的重要风险因素（OR: 1.62-2.50）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习预测MOAKS缺乏不确定性量化，且结构-疼痛关系研究受限于样本量不足和统计建模不够精细，难以在大规模数据中可靠地揭示骨关节炎结构与疼痛的关联。

Method: 1) 深度学习框架预测MOAKS特征并集成保形预测进行不确定性量化，仅保留高置信度预测；2) 采用纵向潜在类别混合模型（LCMM）分析结构异常与四种膝关节疼痛测量之间的关联。

Result: BML的MCC从0.69提升至0.91，CART从0.45至0.80，ME从0.59至0.89；样本量扩展至2,175个膝关节；识别出快速和稳定两种疼痛进展轨迹；快速进展组OR: BML 1.62、CART 1.83、ME 2.50。

Conclusion: BML、软骨丢失和半月板挤压是骨关节炎疼痛和功能进展的重要风险因素，该可解释AI框架为大规模结构-疼痛关系研究提供了可靠方法。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop an interpretable and trustworthy AI framework that combines deep learning based MRI Osteoarthritis Knee Score (MOAKS) prediction with interpretable statistical modeling to study structure-pain relationships at scale using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI). Materials and Methods: We first developed a deep learning framework to predict MOAKS features directly from knee MRIs and incorporated conformal prediction to provide prediction uncertainty quantification. This uncertainty-aware strategy enables explicit filtering of model outputs, retaining only high-confidence MOAKS predictions at the knee level. Second, we applied a longitudinal latent class mixed model (LCMM) to examine associations between key structural abnormalities and four complementary knee pain measurements. Results: Among the three MRI-defined abnormalities (i.e., bone marrow lesions (BML), cartilage loss (CART), and meniscal extrusion (ME)), our framework substantially improved the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) and some other metrics. For example, MCC increased from 0.69 to 0.91 for BML, from 0.45 to 0.80 for CART, and from 0.59 to 0.89 for ME. Using these high-confidence predictions, we expanded the sample size to 2,175 knees for the LCMM analysis. Two distinct pain trajectories were identified (rapid and stable pain progression). The estimated odds ratios (95% CI) for the rapid progression group were 1.62 (1.12-2.35) for BML, 1.83 (1.24-2.70) for CART loss, and 2.50 (1.75-3.57) for ME. Conclusion: These results highlight the importance of these structural abnormalities as risk factors for pain and functional progression in osteoarthritis.

</details>


### [216] [Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
*Ankit Pratap Singh,Xin Su,Phillip Howard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多表问答缺乏推理监督的问题，构建了合成对比推理轨迹数据集，并利用对比偏好优化（CPO）微调开源大模型，在MMQA上取得9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，最高达21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多表问答资源仅提供问题和最终答案，缺乏解释推理过程的监督信号，导致模型难以进行有效的组合推理。

Method: 利用异构大语言模型生成经过验证的正向推理轨迹和合理的负向推理轨迹，构建对比偏好数据集；然后采用对比偏好优化（CPO）对开源大模型进行微调。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B、Mistral-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上，CPO相比标准监督微调取得了9.7%-16.3%的绝对平均提升，MMQA上最高提升21个百分点；消融实验验证了异构生成器增强对比信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 合成对比推理轨迹结合CPO微调能有效提升多表问答的推理能力，生成的轨迹在忠实性、连贯性和对比性上均表现良好。

Abstract: Multi-table question answering requires models to retrieve relevant evidence, link schemas, and perform compositional reasoning across relational tables. Existing multi-table Q&A resources typically provide questions and final answers but lack reasoning supervision that explains how answers are derived. To address this gap, we construct a synthetic contrastive reasoning-trace dataset for MMQA by generating validated positive traces and plausible negative traces with heterogeneous LLMs. We then use the resulting preference pairs to fine-tune open-weight LLMs with Contrastive Preference Optimization (CPO). Across Qwen3-14B, Mistral-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B, CPO achieves absolute average improvements over Q&A supervised fine-tuning ranging from 9.7%-16.3%, with gains up to 21 percentage points on MMQA. Ablations show that heterogeneous positive and negative trace generators strengthen the contrastive signal, and automated as well as human evaluations indicate that the generated pairs are largely faithful, coherent, and meaningfully contrastive.

</details>


### [217] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM评估器在静态输入下判断稳定，但在交互环境中易受人为操控而发生逆转，且这种逆转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名，并伴随事后合理化而非可靠纠错。作者提出评估鲁棒性分数（ERS）量化交互鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-judge评估假设判断是固定输入的稳定属性，但该假设在交互场景下不成立。作者旨在揭示并量化后决策交互对评估结果的可操控性及其实际影响。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval上进行受控实验，采用反基线挑战协议和平衡目标验证协议来分离可逆性与目标导向的操纵效应，并通过ERS分数综合量化反转敏感性与方向效应。

Result: LLM评估器在重复中性重评估下高度稳定，但在针对性后决策挑战下显著可逆；反转会降低与人类偏好的一致性、改变基准排名；权威框架尤其具有破坏性；修正判断常伴随低重叠的正当理由。

Conclusion: 后决策交互是LLM-as-judge评估的一个特有失败模式，评估协议应不仅测量静态一致性，还需测量在挑战下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [218] [Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
*Liangji Zhu,Sanjay Ranka,Anand Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于残差编码的压缩方法（LBRC和NGLR），针对有损压缩中学习型压缩器在高保真度场景下残差修正流速率过大的问题，通过为学习残差设计专用编码表示，在块级NRMSE目标10^-6至10^-4范围内，相比GAE提升压缩比30-60%（LBRC）和10-40%（NGLR），并在高保真度区间优于SZ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAE方法在高保真度场景（块级NRMSE 10^-6至10^-4）下，为满足逐块精度要求而保留的SVD/PCA系数快速增长，导致残差修正流占据主导地位，压缩效率显著下降。

Method: 提出残差中心化视角：学习残差与原始科学场结构不同，应使用专用表示编码。LBRC为确定性免训练流水线，通过自适应量化学习残差至目标NRMSE，采用3D Lorenzo差分、Z字形映射、位平面编码和熵编码进行无损编码。NGLR在此基础上引入因果神经预测器，对整数取整的Lorenzo预测输出归一化偏置，降低剩余残差码的熵，预测器权重序列化计入比特流。

Result: 在E3SM、JHTDB、ERA5数据集上，块级NRMSE目标10^-6至10^-4范围内：LBRC相比GAE提升压缩比30-60%，与SZ竞争力相当；NGLR在LBRC基础上再提升10-40%，在高保真度区间超越SZ。

Conclusion: 面向学习型压缩器残差定制的残差表示能够保留学习压缩的优势，特别是在全局残差修正成为速率主导因素的高保真度场景下。

Abstract: Lossy compression is essential for massive spatiotemporal data from scientific simulations. Learned compressors can achieve high compression ratios at moderate accuracy targets, but their aggregate reconstruction losses do not guarantee accuracy for each block. Existing Guaranteed Autoencoder (GAE) methods add a per-block residual correction by retaining SVD/PCA-style coefficients until the target is met. This works at moderate tolerances, but in the high-fidelity regime with block-level NRMSE from 10^-6 to 10^-4, the number of retained coefficients grows quickly and the correction stream dominates the total rate.
  We propose a residual-centric view: the learned residual is structurally different from the original scientific field and should be coded with a representation designed for that residual. We introduce two residual coders. LBRC is a deterministic, training-free pipeline that adaptively quantizes the learned residual to the target NRMSE and losslessly encodes the resulting integer residual using 3D Lorenzo differencing, zigzag mapping, bit-plane coding, and entropy coding. NGLR adds a causal neural predictor that outputs a normalized bias for an integer-rounded Lorenzo prediction in the same deterministic integer pipeline, reducing the entropy of the remaining residual code while preserving deterministic decoding. The predictor weights are serialized and counted in the bitstream.
  Across E3SM, JHTDB, and ERA5 at block-level NRMSE targets from 10^-6 to 10^-4, LBRC improves compression ratio over GAE by 30-60% and is broadly competitive with SZ. NGLR adds a further 10-40% over LBRC and outperforms SZ in the evaluated high-fidelity regime. These results show that residual representations tailored to learned-compressor residuals can preserve the advantage of learned compression when global residual correction becomes rate-dominant.

</details>


### [219] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon是一个多智能体框架，通过演化蓝图抽象和两阶段协调器，实现了对研究级数学论文的可靠自动形式化，成功形式化了四个Erdős问题中的七个目标定理，证明了258个引理和定理，无任何未完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程研究数学的自动形式化不仅在处理困难引理时失败，在规模化扩展时也会失败：语句漂移、依赖缠结、上下文衰减、局部修复破坏远程工作。现有方法缺乏能够在大规模、长时间的形式化过程中保持目标保真度的耐久框架。

Method: 提出LeanMarathon多智能体框架：核心抽象为演化蓝图（一个同时作为形式证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录的Lean文件）；四个契约范围的智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）协同工作；两阶段协调器（先通过对抗性评审稳定目标保真度，再通过动态叶子向上的并行CI门控轮次完成证明DAG）。

Result: 在两篇近期研究论文（涉及四个Erdős问题#1051、#1196、#164、#1217）上评估，三次自主运行中形式化了全部七个目标定理，无任何sorry，证明了258个引理和定理。

Conclusion: 可靠的AI协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要能够在大规模数学开发中保持目标保真度的耐久框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [220] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw 是一个面向时间序列的智能体框架，为通用 LLM 智能体提供时间序列原生运行时支持，通过集成可执行时态工具、经验驱动的能力演化和情景多模态记忆，实现上下文感知的开放式时间推理，在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域基准测试中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列通常嵌入丰富的上下文中，真实世界的实践者需要端到端的工作流来分析时间动态，而现有的通用 AI 智能体主要操作在文本空间，未能与结构化时间信号完全对齐，缺乏对时间序列原生运行时的支持。

Method: TimeClaw 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 可执行时态工具，用于基于证据的可审计分析；(2) 经验驱动的能力演化，用于创建可重用的分析例程；(3) 情景多模态记忆，用于检索相关推理轨迹。

Result: 在涵盖能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实领域的不同基准测试上，TimeClaw 展现了改进的性能表现。

Conclusion: TimeClaw 通过为通用 LLM 智能体提供时间序列原生运行时支持，解锁了具有上下文信息的开放式时间推理能力，为时间序列分析中的端到端工作流提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [221] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Ozbay,Ida Sim,Tarek Zohdi,George Em Karniadakis,Jack Gallant,Teresa Head-gordon,Yushan Li,Wenxi Deng,Tao Sun,Huiqi Wang,Zhun Wang,Justin Xu,Chris Yuhao Liu,Yafei Cheng,Rongwang Hu,Aras Bacho,Shengcao Shi,Haoran Wu,Zhiyue Wu,Hannah Yao,Zhuoran Yi,Jenny Yu,Rhea Zhan,Hang Zhou,Blake Zhu,Junfan Zhu,Alan Yuille,Yang Liu,Russell Alan Poldrack,Jiachen Li,Zhenglu Li,Molei Tao,Jing Huang,Wenqi Shi,Costas Spanos,Lichao Sun,Chenguang Wang,Orson Xu,Zhen Dong,Hector Gomez,Aylin Caliskan,Ali Emami,Haimin Hu,Zhi Li,Lihui Liu,Murphy Niu,Yi Shao,Jianxin Sun,Mikko Tolonen,Ting Wang,Sanjiv Das,Yanjun Gao,Wenbo Guo,Erika J Schneider,Zhiyong Lu,Mark Mueller,Radha Poovendran,Somayeh Sojoudi,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents' Last Exam (ALE)基准，用于评估AI代理在长期、经济价值高、结果可验证的真实世界任务上的表现。该基准由250+行业专家联合开发，涵盖O*NET/SOC 2018定义的13个行业集群、55个子领域、1000+任务。当前最困难层级平均通过率仅2.6%，表明AI在GDP相关任务上远未饱和。ALE作为动态基准持续扩展，旨在弥合基准成功与经济影响之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
